# Habitica: Gamified Taskmanager (App Store ID 994882113) — Full Review Analysis

**HabitRPG, Inc. · `com.habitrpg.ios.Habitica` · Productivity · open source**

Every one of the **3,445 reviews** in `reviews.jsonl` was read individually, in its original language, and hand-coded. Nothing was sampled, keyword-filtered or skipped. Corpus: **2015-07-07 → 2026-09-06**, **83 storefronts**, **21 languages** (English 2,697, Chinese 211, Russian 116, Portuguese 89, Spanish 73, German 62, Japanese 55, French 51, and 13 more), corpus mean **3.71★**.

---

## How to read this

- Every quantified claim carries **count, percentage, denominator, scope, period, signal band and review IDs**. Signal bands are those in `Store Review Analysis Prompt.md`.
- Theme counts are **non-exclusive**: a review carrying five ideas is counted in five themes. Percentages are *shares of reviews mentioning a thing* and sum past 100%.
- **§10 is a complete per-review index.** Any number here resolves to exact reviews; any review resolves to the findings it supports via §9.J.
- Review IDs appear as `` `1223698024` `` (the `review_id` in `reviews.jsonl`) and as `#1` (position in date order, 1…3,445). Both appear in §10.
- Quotations are verbatim and short. Non-English text is quoted in the original with a translation. `…` marks an elision.
- Global percentages use **all 3,445 reviews**, from every storefront.

### ⚠️ Eight warnings before any number here is used

1. **This is mostly an English-language, US corpus.** us holds 1,481 of 3,445 reviews (42.99%); English is 2,697 reviews (78.29%). Eleven storefronts reach 50 reviews (§6); the other 72 hold 719 between them.
2. **Written reviews are harsher than the star ratings.** Corpus mean 3.71★; 1★ and 2★ together 788 (22.87%). Apple's lookup API (2026-09-14) gives the US listing 4.27★ from 3,324 ratings, against a US corpus mean of 3.76★. People write when something breaks.
3. **Defect reviews come in waves.** A single day, 2021-08-20, holds 49 reviews, 47 of them crash reports (§7, §9.H). Era and year shares are sensitive to these bursts; §7.6 recomputes the headline shares without them.
4. **The corpus spans eleven years and several products.** The rebuilt iOS app launched on 2015-07-07; guilds and the tavern closed in August 2023 (§0.4). A 2016 complaint and a 2026 complaint may describe different software. There is no app-version field; eras are dated from what reviewers say.
5. **Purchase evidence is thin.** `U_PAID` **55** reviews, **1.60%** of 3,445 state a purchase (subscription, gems or donation). No revenue, conversion or retention figure is derivable (§5).
6. **Outcomes are self-reported.** Habits formed, grades and weight are the reviewer's account and are coded as stated.
7. **Star and text sometimes disagree.** `CONTRA_RATING` **46** reviews, **1.34%** of 3,445: 44 are 5★ reviews reporting a defect. They are kept and coded by their text (§4.3).
8. **Codes are hand judgement.** Each line in the classification file quotes the text behind its codes (§1.4); nothing was assigned by keyword or model.

# PART 0 — READ THIS FIRST

The eight findings that should change a decision.

## 0.1 The RPG layer is what people come for, and it works for them

`PR_GAMIFY` **468** reviews, **13.58%** of 3,445 — High-priority signal, mean 4.72★, 2015-07-08 → 2026-08-09. Reviews that praise the game layer average 4.72★ against a corpus mean of 3.71★. The social and collection layers add to it: `PR_PARTY` **158** reviews, **4.59%** of 3,445; `PR_COLLECT` **88** reviews, **2.55%** of 3,445; `PR_PENALTY` **34** reviews, **0.99%** of 3,445 say losing health for missed Dailies is the pressure they need.

The outcome evidence is broad: `U_OUTCOME` **502** reviews, **14.57%** of 3,445 — High-priority signal, mean 4.70★, 2015-07-07 → 2026-09-05. It splits into getting more done (`OUT_PRODUCTIVE` **197** reviews, **5.72%** of 3,445), a better life in general (`OUT_LIFE` **179** reviews, **5.20%** of 3,445), habits built or broken (`OUT_HABIT` **164** reviews, **4.76%** of 3,445) and concrete results (`OUT_RESULT` **27** reviews, **0.78%** of 3,445). `PR_STICKY` **78** reviews, **2.26%** of 3,445 call it the first or only such app they kept using.

> "i stopped biting my nails, eating in the car" — #971, `1812242046`, au, 5★

> "Para pessoas com depressão, como eu, esse aplicativo é incrível" — #1093, `1930536403`, br, 5★
> *(translation: For people with depression, like me, this app is incredible)*

> "目に見える形で点数が入るからやる気がなくてもやろうという気持ちになれる" — #3444, `14515415839`, jp, 5★
> *(translation: Points go in where you can see them, so you want to do it even when you're not motivated)*

> "I still have adhd but now I feel anxious that my team could get dmg because of me not doing the dishes" — #3371, `14180891534`, pl, 5★

#1093 is the most-voted review in the corpus that carries only positive codes (18 votes).

**Evidence:** `1223786048`, `1350667396`, `1431140893`, `1587120495`, `1778259989`, `2424814479`, `3994957638`, `6785143820`, `10383660563`, `14405792064`.

## 0.2 Reliability is the largest complaint, and it arrives in waves after updates

`U_BUG_ANY` **1428** reviews, **41.45%** of 3,445 — High-priority signal, mean 2.81★, 2015-07-10 → 2026-09-06. It is led by crashes, `BUG_CRASH` **415** reviews, **12.05%** of 3,445, and by reviewers who blame a specific update, `BUG_REGRESSION` **201** reviews, **5.83%** of 3,445. Then come tasks that will not check off, save or appear (`BUG_TASK` **218** reviews, **6.33%** of 3,445), lag (`BUG_SLOW` **162** reviews, **4.70%** of 3,445), sign-up failures (`BUG_SIGNUP` **139** reviews, **4.03%** of 3,445, mean 1.55★), server errors (`BUG_SERVER` **126** reviews, **3.66%** of 3,445), lost or reverted data (`BUG_SYNC` **106** reviews, **3.08%** of 3,445) and Dailies that fail to roll over (`BUG_RESET` **93** reviews, **2.70%** of 3,445).

The complaints concentrate in dated waves:

| Wave | Reviews | Crash | Sign-up | Server | Mean ★ | What reviewers describe |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 2016-11 (month) | 72 | 54 | 0 | 1 | 3.14 | crashes on the Rewards page after an update, gold spent with nothing received (#514, #519) |
| 2017-10 (month) | 86 | 45 | 1 | 0 | 3.13 | crash on launch after an update; reinstalling fixed it for some (#989, #993) |
| 2018-02 (month) | 67 | 3 | 13 | 13 | 2.91 | App Store feature on 2018-02-26; new users could not reach the servers to register (#1241, #1244) |
| 2021-08-20 (one day) | 49 | 47 | 0 | 0 | 2.39 | the app closes seconds after opening (#2336, #2338) |
| 2023-09-16 (one day) | 20 | 0 | 2 | 17 | 2.15 | service outage; accounts inaccessible (#2758, #2763) |

> "Crashes after a few seconds on the Rewards page" — #519, `1483848155`, us, 1★

> "The latest 2 versions of the app have ceased to work at all for me. They crash on startup" — #1024, `1835618625`, us, 1★

> "every time I open it, it crashes and auto closes in a matter of seconds" — #2336, `7712740288`, br, 1★

> "the app will tell me it started a new day, but it hasn’t cleared out my dailies" — #3305, `13838933613`, us, 3★

Crash reports fall to 2.1% of reviews from 2024 on, from 16.2% in 2015–2016 (§7.2). The newer defects are quieter and more corrosive: Dailies not resetting, taps landing on the wrong task, widgets not refreshing. Reviewers trade workarounds (`REV_TIP` 33), most often reinstalling.

**Evidence:** `1283121208`, `1483273461`, `1491945962`, `1760588393`, `1836858565`, `2238771865`, `5030135226`, `7714005918`, `7819426772`, `14516534730`.

## 0.3 The iPhone app has trailed the website and Android for a decade

`NEG_PARITY` **141** reviews, **4.09%** of 3,445 — Very strong signal, mean 3.77★, 2015-07-08 → 2026-09-06. Reviewers name features that exist only on the web (challenges, guild management, the market, stats, inbox) and say the web version is faster. 199 reviews (5.78%) mention the website, desktop or browser at all (§9.F). `NEG_IOS_BEHIND` **21** reviews, **0.61%** of 3,445 compare iOS directly with Android: Korean and Ukrainian are available on Android and not on iPhone, Wear OS has a watch app and watchOS does not.

The platform requests follow: `REQ_WIDGET` **68** reviews, **1.97%** of 3,445; `REQ_WATCH` **67** reviews, **1.94%** of 3,445; `NEG_OFFLINE` **55** reviews, **1.60%** of 3,445. Widgets arrived and then became a defect source (`BUG_WIDGET` **22** reviews, **0.64%** of 3,445, all from 2021 on).

> "I strongly recommend use web version on iPad" — #1023, `1833698218`, ru, 5★

> "安卓使用者是人，蘋果不是人喔" — #1845, `4362725195`, tw, 1★
> *(translation: Android users are people, Apple users aren't?)*

> "It doesn’t make sense that Habitica hasn’t released for Apple Watch but Wear Os" — #3115, `12612023060`, us, 4★

> "you can try the web version for a more stable experience" — #3445, `14516534730`, cn, 5★

#3445 is the last review in the corpus (2026-09-06), a 5★ from a long-time user who recommends the website as the workaround for the app.

**Evidence:** `1223779586`, `1349985635`, `1466249150`, `1725291597`, `2512877116`, `3634326234`, `5475871567`, `7715617252`, `10936712408`, `14516534730`.

## 0.4 Removing the community in 2023 cost the most committed users

Two linked events generate the lowest-rated themes in the corpus. The first is a dispute between staff and the volunteer moderators, `STAFF_CONTROVERSY` **19** reviews, **0.55%** of 3,445 — Emerging signal, mean 1.74★, 2022-12-22 → 2024-11-17. The second is the closure of the guilds and the tavern chat on 8 August 2023, `NEG_GUILD_SHUTDOWN` **32** reviews, **0.93%** of 3,445 — Emerging signal, mean 2.25★, 2023-08-02 → 2026-02-26. Together they cover 43 reviews, mean 2.12★; 10 of them (23.3%) say they had used Habitica for years, against 3.86% of all reviews.

The era that contains both, E4 (2022-12 → 2023-12), has the lowest mean rating of any era, 3.37★ (§7.1). The guild complaint has not stopped: the latest is dated 2026-02-26, from a user returning after two years.

> "When workers asked for better conditions, they were treated disrespectfully and many were fired" — #2622, `9423739332`, us, 1★

> "Two main social hubs, the Tavern and guilds, are being shuttered on August 8th" — #2729, `10215290731`, ca, 3★

> "I have ADHD, and I was in a guild that basically acted as a sort of support group" — #3163, `12975896573`, us, 3★

> "The guilds were the soul of this app" — #3289, `13791199455`, us, 1★

#2729 (10 votes) and #2622 (9 votes) rank 11th and 13th by votes of all 3,445 reviews. Community praise (`PR_COMMUNITY`) falls from 4.4% of E3 reviews to 1.0% of E5. Parties survived the closure, and party praise holds at 5.0% of E5 reviews.

**All IDs:** `9423739332`, `9449394666`, `9506002892`, `9506925619`, `9506941856`, `9511384699`, `9573504233`, `9580263092`, `9884066669`, `9906466527`, `10211945336`, `10212702412`, `10215114763`, `10215290731`, `10215484794`, `10216174483`, `10234490255`, `10262190465`, `10270144041`, `10285157806`, `10306026207`, `10364288828`, `10373579222`, `10452609315`, `10636982787`, `10687935116`, `10709635418`, `10745148778`, `10804970164`, `11359478303`, `11374082152`, `11889401733`, `11913429240`, `11961429685`, `11970123567`, `12065256058`, `12233822015`, `12250071419`, `12975896573`, `13028061267`, `13183379650`, `13304303172`, `13791199455`.

## 0.5 Missing languages are the most-voted complaint

`NEG_LANG` **195** reviews, **5.66%** of 3,445 — High-priority signal, mean 3.46★, 2015-08-03 → 2026-07-11. Of the six most-voted reviews in the corpus, 5 come from the Turkish storefront, and 4 of those six ask for a language: Turkish in each case, with 154 votes between them. The single most-voted review (#2850, 89 votes) is a Turkish user telling those reviewers to learn English. In Korea, 40 of 52 reviews (76.9%) are about the missing Korean translation.

> "Ulan İbranice bile var Türkçe yok" — #2700, `10028573967`, tr, 1★
> *(translation: Man, there's even Hebrew but no Turkish)*

> "왜 안드로이드에선 되는데 아이폰은 한국어를 지원하지않을까요" — #2956, `11637734854`, kr, 5★
> *(translation: Why does Android have Korean and iPhone doesn't?)*

> "has been fully translated into Ukrainian for few months" — #3339, `14078142287`, ua, 4★

> "Немає української мови" — #3348, `14126986905`, ua, 1★
> *(translation: There's no Ukrainian)*

The translations are volunteer work (#2975, #3246, #3339). #3339 is a volunteer reporting that the finished Ukrainian iOS translation had not shipped (2026-05-18). Apple's lookup (external, 2026-09-14) now lists 18 languages including Ukrainian and Hebrew, but not Korean or Turkish (§2.2). Partial translation is its own defect: mixed-language screens make up much of `BUG_DISPLAY` in cn (20 of 63).

**Evidence:** `1237532014`, `1627387423`, `2843467785`, `3740152235`, `4248810933`, `6027467301`, `8911097728`, `10098168903`, `12129686613`, `14291468570`.

## 0.6 The free core earns goodwill; the gem economy spends it

Praise for what is free is consistent: `PR_FREE` **68** reviews, **1.97%** of 3,445; `MON_NOT_REQUIRED` **44** reviews, **1.28%** of 3,445 say payment is optional. Against that, `MON_GEM_GATE` **47** reviews, **1.36%** of 3,445 — Meaningful signal, mean 3.34★, 2017-03-12 → 2026-07-16: quests, hairstyles, backgrounds and pet food sit behind gems, which free players can barely earn. `MON_P2W_PERCEIVED` **19** reviews, **0.55%** of 3,445 call it pay-to-win or a money grab (mean 1.84★).

The gem complaint is growing. It is 4.2% of E5 reviews (2024 →), against at most 1.0% in any earlier era.

> "it doesn’t hide functionality behind a paywall" — #3406, `14328108297`, us, 5★

> "You don’t need to pay anything to use it but I chose to subscribe" — #3257, `13622777386`, pl, 5★

> "The quests and many customization items are stuck behind gem paywalls" — #3277, `13735335061`, us, 3★

> "After subscribing you are allotted the ability to buy 24 gems for only $6 more dollars" — #3177, `13121830633`, us, 1★

Stated purchasers are few (`U_PAID` 55) and often unhappy: mean 3.25★, and 15 of 55 report a purchase that went wrong (§5).

**Evidence:** `1338112035`, `2443195146`, `3294545900`, `5549762950`, `8826741115`, `11354560929`, `11970123567`, `13063678816`, `13979172887`, `14308350433`.

## 0.7 The requests are for tracker basics as much as for game content

`U_REQ_ANY` **563** reviews, **16.34%** of 3,445 — High-priority signal, mean 4.10★, 2015-07-10 → 2026-09-01. The share of reviews asking for something rises from 10.7% in E1 to 26.0% in E5, while crash reports fall (§7.2). The largest asks are tracker basics: history and statistics (`REQ_STATS` **71** reviews, **2.06%** of 3,445), a calendar view (`REQ_CALENDAR` **33** reviews, **0.96%** of 3,445), flexible repeat schedules such as "3 times a week" (`REQ_FREQ` **45** reviews, **1.31%** of 3,445), sorting (`REQ_REORDER` **30** reviews, **0.87%** of 3,445) and multiple lists (`REQ_LISTS` **20** reviews, **0.58%** of 3,445). Game-side asks are almost as large: more content, actual battles and an endgame (`REQ_CONTENT` **79** reviews, **2.29%** of 3,445), plus trading (`REQ_TRADE` **12** reviews, **0.35%** of 3,445).

> "if it had a calender for the habits and I could see how many days in a month I completed the task" — #1501, `3169036055`, tr, 4★

> "there being no option for truly weekly tasks" — #3094, `12477691291`, gb, 1★

> "A progressão fica meio estagnada" — #3430, `14438914703`, br, 4★
> *(translation: Progression stagnates somewhat)*

#1501 carries 14 votes, more than any other feature request.

**Evidence:** `1250294239`, `1734640664`, `4246845332`, `5968551579`, `7603445575`, `8914777741`, `10374605623`, `11968297088`, `12872173872`, `14405792064`.

## 0.8 Users with ADHD are the fastest-growing audience, and the most exposed to its flaws

`USE_ADHD` **71** reviews, **2.06%** of 3,445 — Meaningful signal, mean 4.41★, 2016-04-15 → 2026-08-19. The share is several times higher in the latest era: 6.0% of E5 reviews against 1.3%, 0.8% and 1.0% in E1–E3. Other mental-health contexts, depression and anxiety most often, add `USE_MENTAL` **51** reviews, **1.48%** of 3,445. The instant reward of XP and gold is the mechanism these reviewers name.

> "Primera app que me ayuda con mi TDA" — #3229, `13463206718`, cl, 5★
> *(translation: The first app that helps me with my ADD)*

> "I have ADHD and find it hard to do tasks, but this app has been a game changer" — #3429, `14432509862`, us, 5★

The same audience names the failure modes. Pop-ups that derail the task (#3301), punishments that feel arbitrary or absent (#3314), too much stimulus (#3432), and the loss of a guild that worked as a support group (#3163):

> "ADHD, autism, and executive dysfunction, not helpful" — #3314, `13882582042`, us, 2★

> "Demasiados impulsos texto y provoca que te distraigas de las tareas" — #3432, `14446058814`, es, 2★
> *(translation: Too many stimuli and text; it distracts you from the tasks you're doing)*

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| File | Role | Records |
|---|---|---:|
| `App Store Reviews/85. Habitica - Gamified Taskmanager - Stay motivated and organized/reviews.jsonl` | **the corpus**; every claim resolves here | 3,445 |
| `by_country/*.jsonl` | 83 per-storefront files | 3,445 (union) |
| `manifest.json` | extraction metadata, per-country counts, rating distribution | — |
| `_state.json` | collection state per polled storefront | — |
| Apple iTunes Lookup API (external, 2026-09-14) | public rating, listing text, version, languages | 11 storefronts |

## 1.2 Schema actually present

All 13 fields are present on all 3,445 records; no title or body is empty.

| Field | Used for | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `review_id` | primary key | 3,445 distinct; zero duplicates |
| `country`, `country_name` | §6 | 83 storefronts |
| `rating` | §4 | integers 1–5 only |
| `title`, `body` | **read in full for every record** | median body length 171 characters |
| `author` | authenticity tests only (§9.H) | 3 names appear on two storefronts each |
| `date` | §7 | 2015-07-07 → 2026-09-06 |
| `vote_count`, `vote_sum` | §9.H | 406 reviews have votes; max 89 |
| `is_edited` | §9.H | 61 edited |
| `app_id`, `app_name` | constant (`994882113`, `Habitica: Gamified Taskmanager`) | |

**Not present, and therefore never claimed:** app version per review, purchase records, developer responses, device, and any identifier linking a reviewer across storefronts.

`85-reconcile.py` output, verbatim:

```
field presence               : {'review_id': 3445, 'app_id': 3445, 'app_name': 3445, 'country': 3445, 'country_name': 3445, 'rating': 3445, 'title': 3445, 'body': 3445, 'author': 3445, 'date': 3445, 'vote_count': 3445, 'vote_sum': 3445, 'is_edited': 3445}
empty title / body           : 0 / 0

date range                   : 2015-07-07T20:37:59Z -> 2026-09-06T07:03:24Z

is_edited true               : 61
vote_count>0                 : 406, max 89
vote_sum negative            : 0
```

**Reviews read: 3,445/3,445 = 100%. Reviews hand-coded: 3,445/3,445 = 100%.** No deduplication was applied: there are no technical duplicates. One short body, "great app!", recurs across three authors years apart (§9.H); all three are kept.

## 1.4 Processing method

1. `85-dump.py` emits all 3,445 reviews in date order with a stable index (1…3,445).
2. Reviews were read in **35 batches of up to 100**, in order, in full, in the original language. Translations quoted here were checked against the source string.
3. Each review received hand codes plus a note quoting the text that drove them (`85-cls/*.txt`, 3,445 lines).
4. **After every batch**, `85-check-cls.py` re-asserted each line's `review_id` at its index and that every quoted fragment in the note occurs in that review. From batch 2 on, lines were written without IDs and `85-fill-ids.py` filled them from the index, so an ID cannot drift from its text (§9.C).
5. `85-build-classification.py` rejects unknown codes, adds mechanical codes (`STAR_n`, `ERA_En`) and derives 7 unions.
6. `85-validate.py` runs the data checks in §9.C.
7. `85-build-report.py` computes every number and chooses every representative ID from the data at build time. It **refuses to write** if any quotation is not found verbatim in the review it is attributed to, and asserts every trend direction stated in the narrative.
8. `85-verify-report.py` re-reads the written file and checks it against the corpus (§9.M).

**Classification is hand judgement, not an algorithm.** No clustering, keyword rule or model assigned a code.

## 1.5 Signal thresholds

| Share of reviews | Label |
|---|---|
| < 0.1% | Ignore by default |
| 0.1% – < 0.5% | Weak signal |
| 0.5% – < 1% | Emerging signal |
| 1% – < 3% | Meaningful signal |
| 3% – 5% | Very strong signal |
| > 5% | High-priority signal |

At n=3,445, 0.1% ≈ 3 reviews, 1% ≈ 34 and 5% ≈ 172. Segment rates (within a star band, era, storefront or the paid segment) are labelled as segment rates and show both denominators; their band column is a segment band, not a corpus band.

## 1.6 Limitations, stated plainly

- **Selection.** Reviews over-represent moments of breakage (a crash wave, a feature removal) and moments of gratitude. They do not measure how many people use the app quietly.
- **Mixed platforms.** Reviewers often describe the website, Android or the service as a whole. Codes follow what they say; they are not restricted to the iOS client.
- **No version data.** Product events are dated from reviewer statements and the burst pattern, not from a release history.
- **Language mix.** 2,697 reviews are in English. English is also the review language on most small non-English storefronts, so storefront is not a proxy for language (§6.14).
- **Thin late eras.** E4 has 215 reviews; single-month or single-day spikes can move its shares.

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETIZATION MODEL

## 2.1 What the product is, from the reviews

A task manager dressed as a 16-bit role-playing game. Tasks come in three types: **Habits** (+/− counters with no schedule), **Dailies** (scheduled tasks that reset each day and cost health if missed) and **To-Dos** (one-offs). Completing tasks earns experience and gold; gold buys gear or custom real-life rewards the user defines. Characters level up, choose a class, hatch pets and raise mounts, and join **parties** that fight quest bosses together. Missed Dailies damage the whole party. Paying adds gems (the second currency) and cosmetics. Until August 2023 there were public **guilds** and a **tavern** chat; **challenges** remain. The service is open source and also runs on the web and Android.

| Capability | Positive evidence | Friction evidence |
|---|---|---|
| RPG layer: XP, gold, levels, classes | `PR_GAMIFY` 468, `PR_CONCEPT` 285, `SEG_GAMER` 107 | `NEG_GAMEPLAY` 64, `NEG_ART` 35, `NEG_CHILDISH` 3, `NEG_RPG_UNWANTED` 3 |
| Habits / Dailies / To-Dos | `PR_TASKTYPES` 91, `PR_CUSTOM` 66, `PR_ORGANIZE` 95 | `NEG_LEARNING_CURVE` 73, `NEG_TODO_BASIC` 12, `REQ_FREQ` 45, `REQ_DUE` 12, `REQ_REPEAT_TODO` 9 |

| ca | 4.00 | 409 | 193 | 3.45 |
| cn | 4.05 | 366 | 201 | 3.79 |
| ru | 4.32 | 360 | 164 | 3.87 |
| de | 4.10 | 354 | 105 | 3.31 |
| gb | 4.00 | 351 | 153 | 3.42 |

| jp | 4.34 | 268 | 66 | 3.80 |

| kr | 4.18 | 91 | 52 | 3.87 |

- Listing title **Habitica: Gamified Taskmanager**; seller **HabitRPG, Inc.**; Productivity; age rating 12+; released 2015-07-07; current version **4.0.7**, dated **2026-08-20**; price Free; minimum iOS 17.6.
- Declared languages (18): BG, HR, CS, DA, NL, EN, FR, DE, HE, IT, JA, PL, PT, RO, RU, ZH, ES, UK. Korean and Turkish, the two most-requested languages (§3.7), are absent.
- US description (verbatim): "Habitica is an open-source app run by a small team that’s made better by the work of volunteers" and "Habitica can be fully enjoyed for free, but if you would like to help support us, we offer optional in-app purchases and subscriptions."
- Subscription prices stated in the US description: "$4.99 every month; $14.99 every three months; $29.99 every six months; and $47.99 every year".
- The US description still lists **Guilds** among the features. Reviewers report guilds closed in August 2023 (§0.4). The listing text is the developer's and is not used as evidence.
- In all 11 storefronts the public rating count exceeds the corpus review count, and the public mean exceeds the corpus mean by 0.26 to 0.78 stars. Public counts may reflect rating resets and are not comparable over time.

## 2.4 What could not be established

- The in-app purchase list and gem prices by storefront.
- Exact release dates of the crash waves and the 2018 redesign; they are dated here by review bursts.
- Whether the Ukrainian translation that #3339 reported as unshipped (2026-05) is the one now declared in the listing.
- What share of reviewers describe the iOS app rather than the website or the service.

Half the corpus praises something and two in five report a defect: 409 reviews (11.87%) do both. Praise of the concept is especially common in negative reviews: `PR_CONCEPT` mean 3.40★, and 82 of its 285 reviews are 1–2★. "Great idea, broken app" is the most typical low-star shape.

## 3.2 What users value, and why it works for them

### The game layer and the people around it (high-priority)

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 3,445 | Band | Mean ★ | Dates |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|---|
| `PR_GAMIFY` | Gamification makes tasks fun / rewarding | 468 | 13.58% | High-priority signal | 4.72 | 2015-07-08 → 2026-08-09 |
| `PR_MOTIVATION` | Motivating | 214 | 6.21% | High-priority signal | 4.68 | 2015-07-22 → 2026-09-04 |
| `PR_PARTY` | Party / friends / accountability with others | 158 | 4.59% | Very strong signal | 4.72 | 2015-11-25 → 2026-08-29 |
| `PR_COMMUNITY` | Community / guilds / tavern / challenges | 116 | 3.37% | Very strong signal | 4.71 | 2015-07-08 → 2026-08-29 |
| `PR_COLLECT` | Avatar, gear, pets and mounts | 88 | 2.55% | Meaningful signal | 4.81 | 2016-01-02 → 2026-08-29 |
| `PR_REWARDS` | Custom rewards / gold economy | 49 | 1.42% | Meaningful signal | 4.67 | 2015-08-14 → 2026-08-25 |
| `PR_PENALTY` | Damage / consequences motivate | 34 | 0.99% | Emerging signal | 4.53 | 2015-10-21 → 2026-07-29 |
| `PR_PROGRESS_VIS` | Streaks / colour-coding / visible progress | 43 | 1.25% | Meaningful signal | 4.70 | 2015-11-05 → 2026-09-05 |
| `PR_EVENTS` | Seasonal events / world quests / new content | 7 | 0.20% | Weak signal | 4.86 | 2016-06-29 → 2026-07-15 |
| `SEG_GAMER` | Identifies as / addresses gamers | 107 | 3.11% | Very strong signal | 4.70 | 2015-07-22 → 2026-08-15 |
| `SEG_NONGAMER` | Not a gamer but still values it | 21 | 0.61% | Emerging signal | 4.48 | 2016-01-22 → 2026-08-03 |

Three mechanisms recur. **Instant reward**: gold and XP land the moment a task is ticked. **Consequence**: missed Dailies cost health, and in a party they hurt friends. **Collection**: eggs, pets and mounts keep people returning after novelty fades. "I am constantly doing chores just to earn food for them" (#3323, `13979172887`, us, 4★). Parties turn the consequence social: "In my group, we keep each other motivated" (#3028, `12016804932`, us, 5★). Non-gamers find value too (`SEG_NONGAMER` 21): "Best habit tracking app, even without the gamification" (#3419, `14383426803`, us, 5★).

Families use it as a chore system for children (`USE_FAMILY` 58): "I went from nagging my 11 year old son to do tasks. Now he is asking for more" (#3193, `13270474069`, us, 5★). Professionals recommend it to clients (`SEG_PRO` 16): a psychotherapist (#278), a life coach (#3092), an autism/ADHD support worker (#2618), and therapists who suggested it to reviewers (#2835, #3108).

Low-star reviews are mostly defect reviews: 386 of 488 1★ reviews (79.1%) carry a defect code. Sign-up failure is the harshest (mean 1.55★): people who never got in have nothing else to say. "they haven’t been able to program a functional login screen in two years" (#1383, `2784799182`, us, 1★).

**Recent defects (E5, 2024 →).** Ranked by count within E5: `BUG_TASK` 28, `BUG_SLOW` 27, `BUG_SIGNUP` 22, `BUG_RESET` 21, `BUG_SERVER` 19, `BUG_WIDGET` 15, `BUG_ACCOUNT` 14, `BUG_CRASH` 13. Day rollover and widgets rank much higher than in earlier eras; crashes much lower.

**Workarounds.** `REV_TIP` 33: reinstalling (#989, #993, #3138), logging out and in (#1277), using the website (#295, #3445). `REV_UPDATED` 35 reviewers edited their review after a fix or a relapse, e.g. "When that issue is fixed, 5 stars" (#893, `1753724012`, us, 1★).

The watch request never stops: `REQ_WATCH` appears in every era, from 2015-12-07 to 2026-04-29. `NEG_CHINA_NET` **16** reviews, **0.46%** of 3,445 describe the service as unreachable from mainland China without a VPN, from 2015 to 2026: "翻墙连接也不行" *(even using a VPN does not work)* (#1392, `2841387385`, cn, 2★).

| `BUG_SOCIAL` | Party / guild / chat / invite broken | 78 | 2.26% | Meaningful signal | 2.90 | 2015-09-01 → 2026-05-22 |

Community was a draw long before 2023: "Habitica was one of the least toxic social spaces on the internet" (#2731, `10216174483`, us, 1★). It also produced complaints: moderators and party members described as rude or controlling (`NEG_COMMUNITY` 20; #1287, #1316, #2928). After the closure the social requests are for small, safe versions of what was lost: friend lists (#3052, #3176), cheering between party members (#3225), a party finder for solo players (`NEG_SOLO` 16).

Three distinct problems share the code: **no translation at all** (Turkish, Korean, Arabic, Vietnamese); **translated elsewhere but not on iOS** (Korean and Ukrainian on Android and web, `NEG_IOS_BEHIND`); and **broken language switching**, where screens mix languages or revert to English (#2810, #2917, #3168, #3348). Localisation praise is rare (`PR_LANG` 8).

| `NOTE_CHEAT` | Honour system: easy to cheat | 27 | 0.78% | Emerging signal | 4.26 | 2015-12-13 → 2026-02-10 |

Newcomers struggle to tell Habits, Dailies and To-Dos apart: "Why are there 3 sections. Doesn’t make sense" (#3154, `12882490533`, de, 2★). Long-term players say the game runs out: "they are all essentially the same" (#3250, `13581528753`, us, 5★). The death penalty splits users. Some want it softer for illness: "I don’t want to be punished again for my health problems" (#3238, `13501831658`, us, 3★). Others find it meaningless: "there was no consequence for losing “health” by not completing tasks" (#3314, `13882582042`, us, 2★). The honour system is noticed (`NOTE_CHEAT` 27) but rarely treated as a flaw.

Churn statements average 1.84★ and are driven by defects (83 of 124 carry a defect code) and by the community closure (9 of 124). Returners are common (`CHURN_RETURN` 31), often after a hard period: "I crashed out from the penalties when having a tough time and ended up uninstalling it for almost 3 years" (#3402, `14308350433`, us, 4★).

# PART 4 — RATINGS ANALYSIS

## 4.1 The distribution

| ★ | Reviews | % of 3,445 | Mean length of body (chars) |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 5 | 1,523 | 44.21% | 262 |
| 4 | 677 | 19.65% | 266 |
| 3 | 457 | 13.27% | 270 |
| 2 | 300 | 8.71% | 286 |
| 1 | 488 | 14.17% | 203 |
| **mean** | **3.71** | | |

The distribution is bimodal: 5★ 44.21% and 1★ 14.17%, with 4★ (19.65%) larger than 3★ and 2★. Mid-star reviews are the longest: they are usually "love it, but" reviews listing a defect or a request (`REV_CONDITIONAL` 85, of which 57 are 3–4★).

**Reading.** 5★ reviews are about the game layer and outcomes; 4★ reviews already carry crash and parity complaints at high rates (12.6% crash); 3★ and 2★ are defect reviews that still praise the concept; 1★ reviews are crash, sign-up and server failures, and are where deletion is announced (`CHURN_DELETE` 34 of 49).

## 4.3 Rating-versus-text contradictions

`CONTRA_RATING` **46** reviews, **1.34%** of 3,445. 44 are 5★ reviews whose text reports a defect or problem, e.g. "起動してすぐ落ちる" *(it crashes right after launch)* (#1100, `1939410285`, jp, 5★) under 5★. cn is over-represented (9 of 46, against its 5.83% share of the corpus), and 42 of 46 predate 2022-12, consistent with reviewers treating the star field as a contact form. The low-star contradictions are a 1★ given in protest at a rating prompt (#3408) and a 2★ with only praise. All are kept and coded by their text.

**All contradiction IDs:** `1337075973`, `1352479033`, `1430328257`, `1454771753`, `1482903341`, `1483222793`, `1483831043`, `1484525479`, `1484542292`, `1493459679`, `1622846131`, `1665638307`, `1678334746`, `1743621500`, `1782650262`, `1827498416`, `1836858565`, `1933059625`, `1939410285`, `2251321645`, `3103038732`, `3224104773`, `3257750138`, `3456430920`, `3830317628`, `3915877423`, `3976919304`, `3997661661`, `5987173292`, `7654747355`, `7687651425`, `7712076511`, `7714499086`, `7714987893`, `7715069063`, `7715265969`, `7731188985`, `7833192510`, `7850216071`, `8169622671`, `8276001888`, `9169575545`, `9836042595`, `10373353850`, `10374602643`, `14351099577`.

# PART 5 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

## 5.1 The populations, and what they are not

- **Stated purchasers: `U_PAID` 55 (1.60% of 3,445)**, mean 3.25★: say they subscribe or subscribed (`MON_SUB` 47), bought gems (`MON_GEMS` 8) or donated (`MON_DONATE` 2).
- **Stated intent: `MON_WILL_PAY` 36 (1.04%)**, mean 4.06★: say they will or would pay.
- **Stated barrier: 67 reviews (1.94%)**: gem gating, pay-to-win, price or upsell objections.

**None of these is a conversion rate.** The corpus says nothing about how many users pay.

## 5.2 What triggers a purchase

Within the 55 stated purchasers (segment rates):

1. **Support for a free, open, ad-free product**: `MON_SUPPORT_DEV` 13 of 55 purchasers (23.6%); with `MON_NOT_REQUIRED` or `PR_FREE` 8 (14.5%). "I upgraded just to help support the developers" (#265, `1364073113`, ca, 5★)
2. **Long use**: `TENURE_LONG` 12 of 55 (21.8%) against 3.86% of all reviews.
3. **Family and friends**: `USE_FAMILY` 6 of 55; gift subscriptions for friends (#1986, #1990, #3255).
4. **Access to gem items**: `MON_GEM_GATE` 1 of 55 purchasers also object to gem gating; #3177 subscribed and then objected to paying again for gems.

| `PR_FREE` | 5 | 9.1% | 2.0% | 4.61× |
| `SUP_BAD` | 5 | 9.1% | 1.0% | 8.70× |
| `PR_RECOMMEND` | 4 | 7.3% | 6.6% | 1.10× |
| `CHURN_RISK` | 4 | 7.3% | 1.3% | 5.45× |
| `PR_UPDATE` | 4 | 7.3% | 4.9% | 1.47× |

## 5.4 What purchasers report

Purchasers split. The satisfied ones are long-term users who pay to support the project: "I don't mind contributing $5 a month to keep the app and website going" (#209, `1348967667`, us, 5★); "I’m a subscriber now, it’s not expensive" (#2864, `11072292659`, us, 5★). The unhappy ones (15 of 55, plus 2 whose purchase is implied) name specific failures:

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 3,445 | Band | Mean ★ | Dates |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|---|
| `MON_PAID_UNHAPPY` | Paying user dissatisfied | 17 | 0.49% | Weak signal | 1.24 | 2016-03-18 → 2026-04-06 |
| `BUG_IAP` | Purchase not delivered / billing problem | 9 | 0.26% | Weak signal | 1.89 | 2018-01-21 → 2025-06-16 |
| `MON_CANCEL_HARD` | Hard to cancel subscription | 4 | 0.12% | Weak signal | 1.25 | 2021-01-09 → 2026-04-06 |
| `MON_REFUND` | Wants a refund | 1 | 0.03% | Ignore by default | 1.00 | 2024-02-19 → 2024-02-19 |
| `MON_GROUP_PLAN` | Group plan (shared task board) | 4 | 0.12% | Weak signal | 3.25 | 2019-04-02 → 2026-03-09 |

- **Paid, then the app would not open**: "Paid for premium but can barely use it as it constantly crashes" (#1737, `3905147893`, gb, 1★); #1382, #1398.
- **Paid, benefits not credited**: "I got a month subscription and the gems didn’t credit to my account" (#2243, `7056447552`, au, 1★); #1953, #2492, #2606, #3137.
- **Could not cancel**: "Habitica keeps charging for new subscriptions" (#2450, `8088243446`, nl, 1★); #2224, #2441, #3318.
- **Left over the 2023 changes**: "I cancelled my subscription and left the place" (#2748, `10306026207`, nl, 1★); #2643, #3064.

## 5.5 Upgrade barriers among non-buyers

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 3,445 | Band | Mean ★ | Dates |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|---|
| `MON_GEM_GATE` | Objects to gem-only items / wants gems for gold | 47 | 1.36% | Meaningful signal | 3.34 | 2017-03-12 → 2026-07-16 |
| `MON_P2W_PERCEIVED` | Perceived pay-to-win / money trap | 19 | 0.55% | Emerging signal | 1.84 | 2016-02-24 → 2026-06-11 |
| `MON_PRICE_HIGH` | Subscription / gems too expensive | 8 | 0.23% | Weak signal | 2.75 | 2017-08-31 → 2026-06-06 |
| `NEG_UPSELL` | Promotional notifications / upsell | 4 | 0.12% | Weak signal | 2.25 | 2021-07-09 → 2026-03-09 |
| `NEG_CHALLENGE` | Challenge tasks / tags hard to manage | 4 | 0.12% | Weak signal | 4.00 | 2016-03-21 → 2026-07-05 |
| `MON_ALT_SUPPORT` | Wants another way to pay / support | 5 | 0.15% | Weak signal | 4.40 | 2017-07-31 → 2024-11-24 |
| `MON_PRICE_FAIR` | Pricing seen as fair / affordable | 6 | 0.17% | Weak signal | 3.67 | 2021-12-23 → 2026-02-26 |
| `MON_SUB_VALUE` | Subscription perks valued | 3 | 0.09% | Ignore by default | 5.00 | 2020-11-03 → 2025-01-24 |

- **No free path to gems.** The request is not for free gems but for a slow, earned route: streak rewards (#1530, #3277), achievements (#3017), expensive gold-for-gem exchange (#3370). Challenge prize draws are the current route, and reviewers say they reward lying: "you have to just lie and say you did it" (#3277, `13735335061`, us, 3★).
- **Price level.** "12 month usage cost as much as a tripple AAA vid game title" (#1828, `4259179024`, de, 2★); regional pricing in Russia (#3245); "a bit expensive" in Turkey (#2675).
- **Recurring vs one-off.** "I’d gladly pay $10-$15 for the full program but nickel and diming users is off-putting" (#1325, `2487999989`, us, 2★).
- **Donation instead of add-ons.** `MON_ALT_SUPPORT` 5: "then have a donate button not add ons" (#3024, `11987547518`, us, 5★).

## 5.6 Refund and churn drivers after purchase

Only 2 reviews mention a refund (#2441, #2851). The churn drivers among purchasers are the same as for everyone, defects and the community closure: 9 of 55 purchasers state churn, and of those 5 carry a defect code and 3 a governance code.

## 5.7 What cannot be claimed here

- The share of users who pay, renew or cancel.
- Whether gem gating has increased or only complaints about it. The rise in E5 (§0.6) is a rise in mentions.
- Prices by storefront, beyond the US listing and reviewer statements (§9.G).

## 6.2 Eligibility

Storefronts with **≥50 reviews** support standalone claims: us (1481), cn (201), ca (193), ru (164), gb (153), br (129), au (123), de (105), jp (66), fr (59), kr (52). Each gets a section below. Theme tables show storefront segment rates against the global rate; lift = storefront rate ÷ global rate. "Distinctive" themes have lift ≥ 1.8 and at least 5 reviews in the storefront. Storefronts below 50 are in §6.16 as limited evidence.

## 6.3 US — 1,481 reviews, mean 3.76★ — eligible

Languages: English 1478, Russian 2, Chinese 1. 1–2★ 22.6% (global 22.9%). Public rating 4.27★ from 3,324 ratings (external). Dates 2015-07-07 → 2026-09-04.

**Reading:** The US is 42.99% of the corpus and moves every global number. It carries most of the pay-to-win objection (`MON_P2W_PERCEIVED` 15 of 19), the professional recommenders (`SEG_PRO` 13 of 16), the trading requests (`REQ_TRADE` 11 of 12) and the guild closure (`NEG_GUILD_SHUTDOWN` 19 of 32). Its corpus mean (3.76★) sits 0.51 below the public rating.

| `BUG_CRASH` | Crashes / won't open | 7 | 10.6% | 12.0% | 0.88× |
| `PR_COLLECT` | Avatar, gear, pets and mounts | 6 | 9.1% | 2.6% | 3.56× |
| `OUT_LIFE` | Improved / changed my life | 5 | 7.6% | 5.2% | 1.46× |
| `NEG_PARITY` | App lacks website features | 5 | 7.6% | 4.1% | 1.85× |

**Distinctive:** `NEG_HOWTO` 8 (6.52×), `PR_COLLECT` 6 (3.56×), `NEG_LANG` 9 (2.41×), `NEG_PARITY` 5 (1.85×).

**Reading:** 55 of 66 are in Japanese. The distinctive problem is not knowing how to play (`NEG_HOWTO` 8, lift 6.5×): how to get the Orb of Rebirth, how quests start, how to progress. Incomplete Japanese is the second (`NEG_LANG` 9). Collection is loved (`PR_COLLECT` 6).

> *(translation: managing my schedule like a game motivated me more than I expected)*

## 6.14 High-spend and high-review-volume markets

**No consumer-spend dataset was fetched in this session**, so no spend ranking is asserted. Two groups are defined from captured sources:

- **High-spend proxy group** = the six eligible storefronts with the most public ratings in Apple's lookup API (an install-base proxy, not spend): us (3,324), ca (409), cn (366), ru (360), de (354), gb (351).
- **High-review-volume group** = storefronts with ≥100 reviews in this corpus: us, cn, ca, ru, gb, br, au, de.

| Group | Reviews | Mean ★ | Praise % | Outcome % | Defect % | Request % | Language % | Paid % | Gem-gate % |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| High-spend proxy: us + ca + cn + ru + de + gb | 2,297 | 3.70 | 51.7% | 15.4% | 43.4% | 16.4% | 2.7% | 2.0% | 1.7% |
| High-volume: us + cn + ca + ru + gb + br + au + de | 2,549 | 3.71 | 51.8% | 15.7% | 42.7% | 16.3% | 2.8% | 1.8% | 1.6% |
| us only | 1,481 | 3.76 | 56.2% | 18.2% | 41.0% | 16.1% | 0.3% | 2.4% | 2.3% |
| Eligible non-English storefronts (cn, ru, br, de, jp, fr, kr) | 776 | 3.73 | 44.2% | 9.1% | 40.1% | 17.1% | 15.7% | 0.8% | 0.6% |
| Global | 3,445 | 3.71 | 50.8% | 14.6% | 41.5% | 16.3% | 5.7% | 1.6% | 1.4% |

**Reading:** payment and gem-gating signals are concentrated in the English-speaking storefronts (2.4% paid signal in the US against 0.8% in the non-English eligible group), while the non-English storefronts carry the language complaint (15.7% against 0.3% in the US). Defect shares range from 15.4% (kr) to 53.2% (cn).

# PART 7 — TIME-TREND ANALYSIS

## 7.1 Method

Eras are cut at points where reviewers describe a product or governance change, fixed before the era tables were computed. The timeline checks in §9.C assert the boundaries that matter.

- **E1 → E2 at 2017-01-01**: a calendar cut separating the rebuilt app's first eighteen months (launch without Facebook login, the 2016 crash waves) from the 2017–2018 crash and sign-up waves.
- **E2 → E3 at 2019-01-01**: a calendar cut after the mid-2018 redesign and the 2018 sign-up failures.
- **E3 → E4 at 2022-12-01**: the first review describing the staff–moderator dispute (2022-12-22).
- **E4 → E5 at 2024-01-01**: a calendar cut after the guild closure (2023-08-08) and the 2023-09-16 outage.

| Era | Name | Dates | Reviews | Mean ★ | 1–2★ % | 5★ % |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| E1 | Rebuilt app launch | 2015-07-07 → 2016-12-31 | 600 | 4.11 | 13.8% | 58.5% |
| E2 | Crash and sign-up waves | 2017-01-01 → 2018-12-30 | 1,016 | 3.52 | 27.7% | 38.5% |
| E3 | Long middle, pre-dispute | 2019-01-02 → 2022-11-30 | 998 | 3.67 | 23.9% | 41.5% |
| E4 | Moderator dispute, guild closure | 2022-12-03 → 2023-12-26 | 215 | 3.37 | 31.2% | 34.4% |
| E5 | After the community | 2024-01-01 → 2026-09-06 | 616 | 3.83 | 19.2% | 47.6% |

## 7.4 What changed

**E1 (rebuilt app launch, 600 reviews, 4.11★).** The highest mean of any era. The new app is welcomed as better than the old one (`PR_UPDATE` 13.7% of E1) while missing Facebook login (`NEG_FB_LOGIN` 14) and web features (`NEG_PARITY` 7.2%). Praise of the game layer is at its peak (22.8%). Crash waves begin in 2016-05 and 2016-11 (§0.2).

**E2 (crash and sign-up waves, 1,016 reviews, 3.52★).** Defects reach 54.4% of reviews, the highest of any era. Crash-on-launch after updates (2017-09/10), then sign-up and server failures (9.2% sign-up), peaking on the App Store feature day, 2018-02-26 (22 reviews). The mid-2018 redesign removes sorting pets by colour (#1419, #1427, #1449).

**E3 (long middle, pre-dispute, 998 reviews, 3.67★).** Volume falls. Localisation complaints grow (7.5%), sync failures rise (4.9%), and the requests broaden (statistics, content, widgets). The single largest burst in the corpus falls here: 49 reviews on 2021-08-20, 47 of them crash reports.

**E4 (moderator dispute and guild closure, 215 reviews, 3.37★).** The lowest mean of any era. `STAFF_CONTROVERSY` 18 and `NEG_GUILD_SHUTDOWN` 18 appear for the first time; `SUP_NOT_LISTENING` reaches 4.7%; long-term users are 7.9% of reviews. The 2023-09-16 outage adds 20 reviews in a day. Language complaints peak (10.2%). Crashes fall to 2.8%.

**E5 (after the community, 616 reviews, 3.83★).** The defect share is the lowest of any era (26.0%) and the request share the highest (26.0%). New themes: the gem gate (4.2%), ADHD use (6.0%), widget defects (15 of 22), a 2026 redesign that splits users (#3284 and #3293 like it; #3291, #3294 and #3306 do not) and new acquisition routes, among them an AI assistant:

> "I asked ChatGPT for ideas on making my life more like a video game—enter Habitica" — #3122, `12668442218`, us, 5★

## 7.5 What persisted

- **Praise for the game layer**, in every era (7.6–22.8%).
- **Crashes and task defects**, from 2015-11-07 to 2026-09-06.
- **The Apple Watch request**, from 2015-12-07 to 2026-04-29.
- **Turkish and Korean language requests**: 2017-03-20 → 2026-06-15.
- **Web-only features**, `NEG_PARITY` in every era (1.9–7.2%).

## 7.6 Sensitivity — do the bursts drive the conclusions?

The three busiest days (2021-08-20 (49), 2018-02-26 (22), 2023-09-16 (20)) hold 91 reviews.

| Headline share | All 3,445 | Excluding the 3 burst days | Excluding E2 | E4 + E5 only |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `U_PRAISE_ANY` | 50.8% | 51.8% | 51.2% | 45.1% |
| `U_OUTCOME` | 14.6% | 14.8% | 14.2% | 13.0% |
| `PR_GAMIFY` | 13.6% | 13.9% | 12.8% | 8.1% |
| `U_BUG_ANY` | 41.5% | 40.0% | 36.0% | 29.1% |
| `BUG_CRASH` | 12.0% | 10.9% | 10.8% | 2.3% |
| `U_REQ_ANY` | 16.3% | 16.7% | 19.0% | 24.5% |
| `NEG_LANG` | 5.7% | 5.8% | 6.2% | 7.2% |
| `NEG_PARITY` | 4.1% | 4.1% | 4.2% | 2.2% |
| `MON_GEM_GATE` | 1.4% | 1.4% | 1.5% | 3.2% |
| `USE_ADHD` | 2.1% | 2.1% | 2.6% | 5.4% |
| `U_PAID` | 1.6% | 1.6% | 1.6% | 2.0% |

Excluding the burst days or E2 moves no headline share by more than 5.4 points (the largest move is `U_BUG_ANY` without E2). The late period (E4 + E5) differs in kind, not through bursts: far fewer crashes, and more requests, gem-gating and ADHD mentions.

# PART 8 — PRODUCT IMPLICATIONS

Each item cites the findings it rests on. **Immediate fixes** address defects and harms already reported; **experiments** test a hypothesis; **research questions** need data the corpus cannot give.

## 8.1 Immediate fix — a release that cannot break launch or the day rollover (§0.2, §3.3)

- Staged rollout with crash-rate gating. The corpus holds 201 reviews blaming a specific update and single days with 49 reviews. A halted rollout would have contained 2016-11, 2017-10 and 2021-08-20.
- Make "start new day" (cron) idempotent and visible, and never let a failed rollover block ticking tasks (`BUG_RESET` 93; #3305, #3321, #3422).
- Fix tap targets that register on the wrong task (#3126, #3305) and widget refresh (`BUG_WIDGET` 22).

## 8.2 Immediate fix — sign-up and login that never dead-ends (§0.2, §3.3, §3.4)

- `BUG_SIGNUP` 139 (mean 1.55★) and `BUG_ACCOUNT` 85: the onboarding Finish button (#2946, #3307), Google sign-in returning to the same screen (#3211, #3219, #3221, #3426), prefilled usernames that cannot be changed (#3343).
- An offline-first first run, so a server error does not stop someone creating their first task. This also serves mainland China (`NEG_CHINA_NET` 16) and `NEG_OFFLINE` 55.

## 8.3 Immediate fix — ship the translations volunteers already made (§0.5, §3.7)

- Release finished locales in the iOS build as soon as they reach parity on the web and Android (#3339, #3332, #2956), and fix the language setting that reverts to English (#2810, #2917, #3168).
- Publish a translation call for Turkish and Korean inside those storefronts. Reviewers there are offering to help (#2975, #3246) and ask with 176 votes on Turkish-store language reviews.

## 8.4 Immediate fix — purchases that credit, and cancellations that work (§5.4)

- Restore/credit checks for subscription benefits and gems (`BUG_IAP` 9), a visible cancel path and clear App Store subscription management (`MON_CANCEL_HARD` 4), and a preview of paid backgrounds (#2851).

## 8.5 Experiment — a slow, honest path to gems (§0.6, §5.5)

- Replace challenge prize draws as the main free route with a small earned trickle (streaks, monthly check-ins). Hypothesis: it lowers `MON_GEM_GATE` (47, rising) without cutting subscriptions, and removes the incentive to lie that #3277 describes. Measure subscription starts and challenge-completion honesty proxies, not only gem balances.

## 8.6 Experiment — history, statistics and flexible schedules (§0.7, §3.6)

- A per-task history/calendar view and "N times per week" Dailies (`REQ_STATS` 71, `REQ_CALENDAR` 33, `REQ_FREQ` 45). Hypothesis: users who can see progress outside the avatar keep using the app past the point where the game content runs out (#3421, #3430).

## 8.7 Experiment — replace what the guilds did, safely (§0.4, §3.5)

- The closure reviews describe what guilds were for: finding same-language or same-condition peers (#2810, #3163), accountability groups and a party finder (`NEG_SOLO` 16). Test small, opt-in, moderated interest groups or a party-matching queue. Measure party formation and retention of returning users (`CHURN_RETURN` 31).

## 8.8 Experiment — Apple Watch and reliable widgets (§0.3, §3.4)

- A watchOS companion for checking off Dailies and Habits (`REQ_WATCH` 67, every era since 2015; a Wear OS version exists per #2605, #3115) and a Habits widget (#2963, #3244, #3312).

## 8.9 Experiment — an illness mode and a gentler death (§3.8)

- Make resting in the inn discoverable and time-limited, and test milder death penalties such as keeping levels (`NEG_PUNISH` 23, `BUG_DEATH` 12; #3238, #3350, #3387). Hypothesis: fewer punishment-triggered quits (#3402) without losing the pressure that `PR_PENALTY` 34 reviewers value.

## 8.10 What the corpus says to keep

- The RPG layer, parties and the collection loop (§0.1, §3.2).
- A free core with no ads; reviewers pay because of it (§3.2, §5.2).
- The three task types and the depth of customisation (§3.2).
- The web version as a stable fallback until the app matches it (§0.3).

## 8.11 Research questions the corpus cannot answer

1. What share of new installs fail sign-up, and on which step and network?
2. How did retention and subscriptions move after 2023-08-08 for users who had been in a guild?
3. How often does day rollover fail per active user, and on which iOS versions?
4. Do users with ADHD retain differently, and which features do they use?
5. How many active users are in Turkey and Korea, the two storefronts asking loudest for a translation?

# PART 9 — EVIDENCE APPENDIX

## 9.A Counting rules

1. **Denominator.** Global = 3,445. Star band, era, storefront and purchaser denominators are stated at each use.
2. **Non-exclusive.** A review is counted once in each theme it carries. Percentages sum past 100%.
3. **Codes come from the text, never from the star rating.** A 5★ whose text is a crash report is coded as a defect and flagged `CONTRA_RATING`.
4. **Requests vs defects.** "It doesn't have X" is `REQ_*`; "X doesn't work" is `BUG_*`; "X is only on the website" is `NEG_PARITY`; a missing language is `NEG_LANG`; a price or paywall objection is `MON_*`.
5. **`MON_SUB`, `MON_GEMS` and `MON_DONATE` require a statement of purchase.** Intent is `MON_WILL_PAY`. `MON_SUPPORT_DEV` requires paying, or intending to pay, as support.
6. **`BUG_REGRESSION` requires the reviewer to tie a defect to an update.** It is added on top of the defect code.
7. **`OUT_*` outcomes are first-person.** Anticipation is `JUST_STARTED`.
8. **Unions are derived, never hand-applied**; mechanical codes (`STAR_n`, `ERA_En`) come from the record.
9. **Signal bands** are computed from count ÷ denominator using §1.5.

## 9.C Validation — 816 checks, 0 failures

`85-validate.py` output, verbatim:

```
816 checks, 0 failures
  coverage: 8
  codes: 4
  theme_map: 549
  unions: 14
  mech: 6
  timeline: 6
  semantic: 7
  lang: 22
  bounds: 200
```

The timeline checks were written before Part 7 and would fail if its era narrative were back-fitted: no Facebook-login complaint after 2018; no staff-dispute review before 2022-12; no guild-closure review before 2023-07; no widget defect before iOS 14; the App Store feature only in 2018; no ChatGPT mention before its release. The semantic checks require every 1–2★ review to carry a negative, request, failure or noise code, and every 4–5★ `CONTRA_RATING` to carry a negative code.

### What earlier runs caught

1. **One drifted review ID.** #188 carried #189's ID; the alignment check caught it. After that, lines were written without IDs and filled from the index (§1.4).
2. **Paraphrased quotations.** Seven notes quoted loosely (e.g. #1017, #2314, #2391, #2597, #3431); rewritten to the exact text. #2003 is written in Unicode small capitals and its note carries no quote.
3. **An undefined code** (`REQ_OFFLINE` on #62) was rejected by the build and recoded as `NEG_OFFLINE`.
4. **Semantic checks corrected six judgements**: a 1★ protest at the stat-editing loophole lacked a negative code (#1575); a 5★ bug report was flagged `CONTRA_RATING` without a defect code (#1820); a sarcastic 5★ titled "This app is terrible" was wrongly flagged `CONTRA_RATING` (#2543); "urge others to support the developers" was coded as the reviewer's own support (#1864); and two 1★ reviews that open with "love this app" lacked the praise code (#295, #2354).

### What validation cannot check

Whether a code is the *right* judgement. That is auditable only by reading the note on each line of `85-cls/`, which quotes the evidence.

See §7.6. Further stress tests remove reviews most likely to be noise:

| `CONTRA_RATING` | 46 | 1388 of 3,399 (40.84%) | 1745 of 3,399 (51.34%) | 562 of 3,399 (16.53%) |
| The 3 burst days | 91 | 1343 of 3,354 (40.04%) | 1736 of 3,354 (51.76%) | 560 of 3,354 (16.70%) |
| All bodies of 20 characters or fewer | 196 | 1356 of 3,249 (41.74%) | 1676 of 3,249 (51.59%) | 545 of 3,249 (16.77%) |

Reading: $4.99 a month (#3289) and $5 a month (#209, #2545) match the listing's monthly price; $15 every three months (#2795, #3064) matches the quarterly price; $30 every six months (#1299, 2018). #912 (2017) compares about $48 a year for Habitica with about $30 for Todoist. #3177 describes buying 24 gems for $6 on top of a subscription; #2618 bought 60 gems for $10 in a sale; #1763 paid $9 for a group plan and #2885 considered a $9 group plan with $3 extra; #3075 objects to paying $2 for a hairstyle; #1325 would pay $10–15 once rather than microtransactions.

## 9.H Burst, solicitation and authenticity checks

| Check | Result | Reading |
|---|---|---|
| Repeat author names | 3: ChibiSara (#1070 jp, #1082 us); Ghudoosh (#1430 gb, #2407 sa); Norbyscook (#2102 us, #3019 ca) | same name on two storefronts, 10 to 1558 days apart; bodies differ |
| Duplicate bodies (≥5 chars) across accounts | 1 group: #366 #2390 #3265 | "great app!", years apart; not a pattern |
| Busiest months | 2017-08 (87), 2017-10 (86), 2018-01 (78), 2021-08 (76) | crash reports per month: 2017-08 13, 2017-10 45, 2018-01 8, 2021-08 59; 2021-08 is mostly one day (49 on 2021-08-20) |
| Busiest days | 2021-08-20 (49), 2018-02-26 (22), 2023-09-16 (20), 2018-01-19 (16), 2017-08-24 (15) | 2021-08-20: 47 crash reports; 2018-02-26: App Store feature (`MKT_FEATURED` 4) and server failures; 2023-09-16: outage |
| Reviews with votes | 406 (11.79%) | max 89 (#2850); top six: #2850 (89), #1255 (60), #1677 (46), #996 (27), #1689 (22), #2700 (21) |
| Edited reviews | 61 | `REV_UPDATED` 35 say in the text that they changed their review |
| Complaints about rating prompts (`NEG_REVIEW_NAG`) | 2 | #792 (2017), #3408 (2026, 1★ in protest) — a prompt exists; no sign of incentivised reviews |
| Reviewers discussing other reviews (`REV_ABOUT_REVIEWS`) | 3 | #2850, the most-voted review, argues with Turkish-language requesters |
| `CONTRA_RATING` | 46 | §4.3 |

**Reading:** no evidence of seeded or coordinated reviews. The bursts are defect waves and one App Store feature day; their content matches their dates. The corpus's negativity relative to the public rating is consistent with people writing when something breaks.

## 9.I Developer responsiveness — the evidence both ways

Developer replies are not in the dataset. Reviewers describe support: `PR_SUPPORT` **26** reviews, **0.75%** of 3,445 praise it, e.g. "the development team was super responsive on email to a bug" (#3141, `12793687192`, us, 5★); `SUP_FIXED` **7** reviews, **0.20%** of 3,445 report a fix, e.g. "I sent a complaint and they just now fixed it" (#3433, `14452093158`, us, 4★). On the other side, `SUP_BAD` **36** reviews, **1.04%** of 3,445 report rude, absent or unhelpful support ("they were rude saying just use the app itself for now" (#3322, `13971265948`, us, 1★)) and `SUP_NOT_LISTENING` **32** reviews, **0.93%** of 3,445 say long-reported bugs and requests go unaddressed, rising to 4.7% of E4.

## 9.L Reproduction

```
sh Temp/85-run-all.sh
```

Runs dump → reconcile → alignment check → build classification → validate → build report → verify, then build and verify again so §9.M carries the final verification output.
